    Corporate Profile


    申洲國際集團控股有限公司（「申洲國際」）及                                Shenzhou Inter national Group Holdings Limited (“Shenzhou
    其附屬公司（ 統稱「本集團」）為全球最大的縱                               International”) and its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”) is one of
    向一體化針織製造商之一，主要以代工(OEM)                               the largest vertically integrated knitwear manufacturer in the world.
    及 委 託 設 計(ODM)相 結 合 方 式 為 客 戶 製 造 質                  The Group is principally engaged in the manufacturing of high-end
    量 上 乘 的 針 織 品。根 據 中 國 服 裝 家 用 紡 織 品                  knitwear for the customers by the method of combining OEN and
    貿易發展報告和中國紡織品服裝對外貿易報                                  ODM. The Group was also the knitwear garment manufacturing
    告，本 集 團 是 中 國 出 口 金 額 的 針 織 服 裝 生 產                  enterprise with one of the largest exporting amount of knitwear and
    企業及中國服裝出口企業排名中名列前茅。                                  ranked among the top PRC garment exporting enterprises according
                                                         to the report on the development of China’s home textile trade in the
                                                         garment industry and the report on China’s foreign trade in textiles
                                                         and garment.

    本集團的生產基地位於寧波市經濟技術開發                                  The Group’s production base is located in Ningbo Economic &
    區 和 越 南，並 策 略 性 地 在 中 國 衢 州 市 及 安 慶                  Technical Development Zone and Vietnam, and the Group has
    市、越 南 胡 志 明 市 和 柬 埔 寨 金 邊 市 設 有 製 衣                  strategically set up garment factories in Quzhou and Anqing of
    工廠，在上海、香港及大阪均設有銷售辦事處                                 China, Ho Chi Minh City of Vietnam and Phnom Penh of Cambodia.
    或代表處。現時本集團員工逾82,700人，廠房                              The Group also established sales offices or representative offices
    佔 地 面 積 逾 五 百 二 十 五 萬 平 方 米，建 築 面 積                  in Shanghai, Hong Kong and Osaka. The Group currently has over
    逾 三 百 三 十 二 萬 平 方 米，每 年 生 產 各 類 針 織                  82,700 employees. The area of the factories is over 5,250,000 sq.m.
    服裝約4億件。                                              and floor area covers over 3,320,000 sq.m., which can produce
                                                         various knitwear of about 400 million pieces every year.

    本 集 團 主 要 生 產 針 織 運 動 類 用 品、休 閒 服 裝                  The Group focuses on producing sports wear, casual wear and
    及內衣服裝，主要客戶包括NIKE、UNIQLO、                             lingerie wear for major international renowned clients including
    ADIDAS及PUMA等 國 際 知 名 品 牌 客 戶。產 品                     NIKE, UNIQLO, ADIDAS and PUMA. The products’ markets are
    市 場 遍 佈 中 國 內 地、日 本、亞 太 區 及 歐 美 市                    widely spread in the PRC, Japan, Asia Pacific, Europe and the US.
    場。

    標，申洲國際已於二零零五年十一月成功在香                                 Exchange of Hong Kong Limited in November 2005 with an aim to
    港 聯 合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司 主 板 上 市。本 公 司 股                  be the most competitive knitwear manufacturer in the world. The
    份代號為2313，並於二零一零年三月八日起                                stock code of the Company is 2313. Shenzhou International was
    獲納入恒生綜合指數、恒生綜合行業指數（ 消                                included in Hang Seng Composite Index, Hang Seng Composite
    費品）及恒生綜合中型股指數成份股。自二零                                 Industry Index (Consumer Goods) and Hang Seng Composite
    一三年六月一日起，申洲國際被納入MSCI明                                MiddleCap Index, all effective from 8 March 2010. Shenzhou
    晟指數。同時，於二零一五年九月份起獲納入                                 International has also been included in MSCI Index since June 1

    於二零一八年三月五日起正式獲納入成為恒                                  Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability Index Series since September

     管理層討論及分析
     Management Discussion and Analysis




     經營環境                                                 BUSINESS ENVIRONMENT
     二零一八年度，全球經濟總體延續復甦態勢，                                 In 2018, the global economy continued its recovery in general.
     主要發達經濟體美國之經濟增長較為強勁，                                  Among all the developed economies, the economic growth in the
     歐元區經濟增長勢頭持續放緩，日本經濟波動                                 US was much stronger, the momentum of economic growth in
     性增大，海外市場消費需求穩中有升；全球貿                                 the Euro Zone remained sluggish, the volatility of the economy in
     易環境更趨複雜，尤其中美兩大經濟體間之貿                                 Japan became stronger and the consumption demand in overseas
     易摩擦成為全球貿易和經濟增長之不確定性                                  markets was stable and rising. The global economic environment

     作 為 本 集 團 整 體 理 財 政 策 的 一 部 分，本 集 團                  As a part of overall treasury management policies of the Group, the
     向 多 間 中 國 的 持 牌 銀 行 購 買 金 融 產 品（ 包 括                 Group purchased financial products from licensed banks of China
     抵押存款、其他金融資產、按公允值計量且變                                 (including pledged deposits, other financial assets, financial assets
     動 計 入 損 益 之 金 融 資 產、結 構 存 款 及 定 期 存                  at fair value through profit or loss, structured deposits and fixed
     款），透過合法且低風險的管道最大化本集團                                 deposits) to maximize the return brought by idle money of the Group
     閒置資金帶來的回報。有關購買該等金融產品                                 through legal and low-risk channel. The results for applicable size
     的相關規模測試結果均低於5%，故而該等購                                 test about purchasing the financial products was lower than 5%,
     買毋須遵守上市規則第十四章須予公佈的交                                  thus, this purchase was not subject to the notifiable transaction
     易 之 規 定。購 買 該 等 金 融 產 品 已 獲 董 事 會 就                  requirements under Chapter 14 of the Listing Rules. The purchase of
     監察本公司庫務管理政策執行情況而成立之                                  such financial products was approved by the investment and lending
     投 資 及 借 貸 委 員 會 批 准。該 等 金 融 產 品 之 詳                  committee established by the Board based on implementation of
     情參閱本年年報之財務報表所載之附註21、                                 treasury management policies of the Company. Details of those
     22、23及24。                                            financial products can be found in notes 21, 22, 23 and 24 to the
                                                          Financial Statements contained in this annual report.

     企業管治                                                 CORPORATE GOVERNANCE
     本 集 團 一 貫 的 目 標 是 努 力 提 升 企 業 價 值，以                  The Group’s stated objective is to enhance its corporate value,
     淨利潤的良好增長及現金流量的長期穩定為                                  focusing on the solid growth in net profit and consistently
     核心，確保本集團的長期持續發展，為股東帶                                 stable cash flow, to ensure the Group’s long-term, sustainable
     來 良 好 的 回 報。本 集 團 矢 志 提 高 企 業 管 治 水                  development and to achieve sound returns for shareholders. The
     平，並全力增加透明度。通過持續為董事與員                                 Group is committed to raising its corporate governance standards
     工 提 供 培 訓 及 外 聘 專 業 顧 問，本 公 司 將 不 斷                  and increasing the transparency of its operations. Such objective
     提升企業管治素質，進而達致以上的目標。                                  will be achieved by constantly improving the quality of corporate
                                                          governance of the Company through the provision of continuous
                                                          training for Directors as well as staff and the appointment of external
                                                          professional advisers.


     董事會自二零零五年十月九日起已採納本身                                  The Board adopted its own Code of Corporate Governance, which
     的企業管治守則，其涵蓋香港聯合交易所有限                                 covers all of the code provisions and most of the recommended
     公司（「聯交所」）證券上市規則附錄十四《企                                best practices of the Code on Corporate Governance Practices (the
     業管治常規守則》（「企業管治守則」）所載的                                “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to the Rules Governing the
     全部守則條文，以及大部分的建議最佳常規守                                 Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited
     則。本 公 司 於 截 至 二 零 一 八 年 十 二 月 三 十 一                  (the “Stock Exchange”) since 9 October 2005. The Company had
     日止年度內一直遵從企業管治守則內的所有                                  complied with all the code provisions of the CG Code throughout the

     本集團致力通過董事會來提升公司效率及利                                  The Group endeavours to enhance corporate efficiency and
     潤 水 平。全 體 董 事 深 知 彼 等 共 同 及 個 別 對 股                  profitability through the Board. The Directors recognise their
     東所負的責任，並勤勉盡職，為本公司的理想                                 collective and individual responsibility to the shareholders and
     業績及股東的最大回報作出貢獻。                                      perform their duties diligently to contribute to positive results for the
                                                          Company and maximize returns for shareholders.

     關關係。                                                 to 132. There are no other material financial, business or relevant

     董 事 會 負 責 本 公 司 的 管 治 工 作，並 負 責 管 理                  The Board is responsible for governing the Company and managing
     股 東 所 委 託 的 資 產。董 事 會 的 主 要 職 責 包 括                  assets entrusted by the shareholders. The principal responsibilities
     制 訂 本 集 團 的 業 務 策 略 方 針、設 定 管 理 層 目                  of the Board include formulating the Group’s business strategies and
     標、監管其表現及評估管理策略的有效性。                                  management objectives, supervising the management and evaluating
                                                          of the effectiveness of management strategies.


     本集團的日常業務管理由執行董事或各部門                                  The day-to-day management of the Group’s businesses is delegated
     的 高 級 主 管 負 責，而 本 集 團 會 定 期 檢 討 彼 等                  to the Executive Directors or officers in charge of each division. The
     獲 授 的 職 能 及 權 力，確 保 彼 等 仍 適 合 履 行 職                  functions and power that are so delegated are reviewed periodically
     務。董 事 會 則 負 責 處 理 影 響 本 集 團 整 體 策 略                  to ensure that they remain appropriate. Matters reserved for the
     政策、財務及股東的事務，包括財務報表、股                                 Board are those affecting the Group’s overall strategic policies,
     息政策、重大會計政策變動、重大合約及主要                                 finances and shareholders including financial statements, dividend
     投 資 等。各 董 事 會 成 員 均 可 分 別 獨 立 接 觸 本                  policy, significant changes in accounting policy, material contracts
     集團的高級管理層，以履行彼等的職責；亦可                                 and major investments. All Board members have separate and
     及 時 查 閱 所 有 有 關 本 集 團 的 資 料，並 可 得 悉                  independent access to the Group’s senior management to fulfill their
     本集團最新的經營狀況、業務活動及發展。董                                 duties. They also have full and timely access to relevant information
     事 會 成 員 亦 可 要 求 徵 詢 獨 立 專 業 意 見，費 用                  about the Group and are kept abreast of the operating condition,
     由本集團承擔。                                              business activities and development of the Group. Independent

董事會明白本身有責任編製真實公允的本公                   The Board acknowledges its responsibility to prepare the Company’s
司 賬 目，並 根 據 香 港 會 計 師 公 會 公 佈 而 普 遍   accounts which give a true and fair view in accordance with the
被 採 用 的 香 港 會 計 準 則 編 製 賬 目。本 公 司 選   accounting standards generally accepted in Hong Kong as published
擇 合 適 的 會 計 政 策 並 貫 徹 採 用，所 作 判 斷 及   by the HKICPA. The Company has selected appropriate accounting
估 計 均 屬 審 慎 及 合 理。董 事 致 力 在 財 務 報 告   policies and has applied them consistently based on prudent and
上對本集團的現有狀況及前景作出中肯及易                   reasonable judgments and estimates. The Directors endeavoured to
於理解的評估。                               make a fair and comprehensible assessment of the Group’s current
                                      conditions and prospects in the financial report.


風險管理及內部監控                             RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                                      CONTROLS
董事會確認其對風險管理及內部監控制度及                   The Board recognises its responsibility of risk management and
檢 討 其 成 效 之 責 任。董 事 會 明 白 本 身 有 責 任   internal control system and reviewing its effectiveness. The Board
評估及釐定本公司為達成戰略目標所願承擔                   acknowledges its responsibility to assess and determine the nature
的 風 險 性 質 及 程 度，並 維 持 一 個 適 當 及 有 效   and degree of risks that it is willing to take in achieving strategic
的 風 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統，以 及 確 保 能 盡   goals as well as to maintain an appropriate and effective risk
早向股東及公眾人士如實報告本公司的業務                   management and internal control system and to ensure accurate
狀況。                                   reporting of the Company’s state of affairs to shareholders and the
                                      public in a timely manner.

本集團的風險管理及內部監控系統旨在管理                   The Group’s risk management and internal control system is
及提高營運效益與效率、確保資產不會被不當                  designed to manage and enhance operating effectiveness and
挪用及未經授權處理、維持恰當的會計記錄及                  efficiency, to safeguard assets against misappropriation and
真 實 公 允 的 財 務 報 告，並 同 時 確 保 遵 守 相 關   unauthorized disposition, to maintain appropriate accounting
的 法 律 及 規 例。該 系 統 會 就 是 否 存 在 重 大 錯   records and financial reports that are true and fair, and to ensure
誤陳述或損失，作出合理但不絕對的確定，並                  compliance with relevant laws and regulations. The system aims
會管理而非消除與其業務活動有關的風險。                   at providing a reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against
                                      material misstatement or loss and managing rather than eliminating
                                      operational risks.

本公司的整體風險管理過程融於本集團的日                   The overall risk management process of the Company is integrated
常運營中，管理層獲委派分析、識別、監控、                  in the day-to-day operations of the Group and the management
評估及應對與本集團業務活動及運營有關的                   is entrusted with duties to analyze, identify, monitor, evaluate
風險。管理層評估本公司可接受的風險水平，                  and respond to risks associated with the business activities and
設立並制定應急計劃，降低不可預測事件的影                  operations of the Group. The management will evaluate risk levels
響，將其發現向審核委員會及董事會報告。審                  acceptable for the Company, set up contingency plans and formulate
核委員會及董事會最終釐定本公司達成業務                   contingency plans to minimize impact of unpredictable events and
目標時所願意接納的重大風險性質及程度，                   report its findings to the Audit Committee and the Board, who
並指引本集團的風險管理策略。                        ultimately determine the nature and extent of significant risk that the
                                      Company is willing to take in achieving its business objectives and
                                      direct the Group’s risk management strategies.

   3.3   智能吊掛系統                      3.3   Intelligent hanging system
         本集團一直推崇精益生產，「智能                   The Group has always upheld lean manufacture. The
         吊掛系統」由電腦控制，負責自動                   “intelligent hanging system” is controlled by computers
         將生產任務分配給各個工作站作                    and is a transmission and logistic tool for allocating
         業員作業的傳遞物流工具，降低了                   production tasks to operators at each workstation. The
         作業員工勞動強度，減少各工序間                   system lowers the heavy workload of operating staff and
         傳遞時間，提高生產效率2-3%。                  reduces the transmission time of each working process,
                                           which increases the production efficiency by 2 to 3%.

        3.5    臭氧洗水                                            3.5   Ozone Wash
               G2 Ozone Wash，即臭氧洗水，臭                                 G2 Ozone Wash means ozone washing. Ozone is a
               氧 是 強 氧 化 劑，通 過G2合 成 的 臭                              strong oxidant which discolour the clothes naturally
               氧對服裝進行洗水，使衣物自然褪                                       and forms vintage style by washing the clothes with
               色而成懷舊風格，通常這一效果要                                       ozone composing of G2. Generally this effect needs
               通 過 化 學 助 劑 來 完 成，而G2生 產                              to be completed by chemical agents, however, G2
               消除了化學品並減少了用水，最小                                       production replaces the use of chemicals and reduces
               化了這類產品對環境的壓力。                                         water consumption, which minimizes the pressure on
                                                                     environment caused by this type of products.

               要和崗位特點另行規定。                                           production and operation and characteristic of the job.

               由早上8點開始工作，到下午5點下                                      starts at 8 a.m. and finishes at 5 p.m. and the normal
               班，而正常工作時間一般不會超過                                       working hour is less than 8 hours. If overtime work is
               8小 時。如 需 要 員 工 加 班，員 工 每                              needed, the daily overtime will be less than 3 hours and
               日 加 班 不 會 超 過3小 時，而 每 月 加                             the monthly overtime will be less than 36 hours.
               班則不會超過36小時。


               休息時間：                                                 Time for rest:


               本集團的員工休息時間一般由中                                        Time for rest of the Group’s employees is generally from

               少休息一天。                                                one day off per week.

7.   反貪污：                            7.   ANTI-CORRUPTION:
     本集團創辦人兼前主席馬寶興先生及各                    In over two decades of Shenzhou Group’s history, the founder
     高管人員在申洲集團成立二十多年以                     and former chairman of the Group, Mr. Ma Baoxing, and each
     來，一貫秉持「誠信」，並將其融入企業                   senior management member have always been focusing on
     文 化。誠 信 是 本 集 團 之 行 為 守 則，亦 是        “integrity” in establishing the corporate culture. Integrity is the
     其道德標準。                               code of conduct and moral standard of the Group.


     早 在 二 零 零 零 年 初，馬 先 生 揮 筆 寫 就        Back in early 2000, Mr. Ma wrote the two Chinese characters
     「誠 信」二 字，並 懸 掛 於 大 廳 中 央，警 醒         “誠信 (integrity)” and hung them in the centre of the lobby,

           括在企業可持續發展表現最卓越                          with the most outstanding performance in terms of

關鍵審計事項（ 續）                                                  KEY AUDIT MATTERS (continued)

關鍵審計事項                                                      關鍵審計事項在審計中是如何應對的
Key audit matter                                            How our audit addressed the key audit matter

應收賬款及票據減值風險（ 續）
Impairment risks for trade and bills receivables
(continued)


Management performed periodic assessment on
the recoverability of the trade receivables and
the sufficiency of provision for impairment based
on information including credit profile of different
customers, ageing of the trade receivables,
historical settlement records, subsequent settlement
status, expected timing and amount of realisation
of outstanding balances, and on-going trading
relationships with the relevant customers. Management
also considered forward-looking information that may
impact the customers’ ability to repay the outstanding
balances, in order to estimate the expected credit
losses for the impairment assessment. No credit loss
allowance was provided based on management’s
impairment assessment.
管理層基於資料（ 包括不同客戶信貸狀況透明度、應收
賬 款 賬 齡、歷 史 結 算 記 錄、後 續 結 算 狀 況、未 償 還 結 餘
變現的預期時間及金額及與相關客戶的持續貿易關係）
對應收賬款的可收回性進行定期評估。管理層亦考慮可
能影響客戶償還未償還結餘的能力的前瞻性資料，以估
計用於減值評估的預期信貸虧損。根據管理層的減值評
估，並無計提信貸虧損撥備。


A high level of management judgement was required
when assessing the impairment of trade and bills
receivables under the expected credit losses model.
於 按 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 模 式 評 估 應 收 賬 款 及 票 據 減 值時需
要高水平管理層判斷。


The accounting policies and disclosures for trade and
bills receivables are included in notes 2.4 and 19 to the
consolidated financial statements.
有 關 應 收 賬 款 及 票 據 的 會 計 政 策 及 披 露 載 於 綜 合財務
報表附註2.4及19。

貴 公 司 董 事 需 對 其 他 信 息 負 責。其 他 信 息 包   The directors of the Company are responsible for the other
括 載 於 年 報 的 信 息，但 不 包 括 綜 合 財 務 報 表   information. The other information comprises the information
及吾等的審計師報告。                            included in the Annual Report, other than the consolidated financial
                                      statements and our auditor’s report thereon.


吾等對綜合財務報表作出的意見並不涵蓋其                   Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not cover
他 信 息，吾 等 亦 不 就 此 發 表 任 何 形 式 的 鑒 證   the other information and we do not express any form of assurance
結論。                                   conclusion thereon.


就 吾 等 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 的 審 計 而 言，吾 等 的   In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,
責 任 為 閱 讀 其 他 信 息，從 而 考 慮 其 他 信 息 是   our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,
否與綜合財務報表或吾等在審計過程中所獲                   consider whether the other information is materially inconsistent with
悉 的 資 料 存 在 重 大 不 符，或 者 似 乎 有 重 大 錯   the consolidated financial statements or our knowledge obtained in
誤陳述。基於吾等已執行的工作，倘吾等認為                  the audit or otherwise appears to be materially misstated. If, based
其 他 信 息 存 在 重 大 錯 誤 陳 述，吾 等 需 要 報 告   on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is a material
有關事實。就此，吾等並無任何報告。                     misstatement of this other information, we are required to report that
                                      fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.

                                                             STATEMENTS
      吾等的目標乃對整體綜合財務報表是否不存                                    Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether
      在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取                                    the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from
      得 合 理 保 證，並 出 具 載 有 吾 等 意 見 的 審 計 師                    material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an
      報告。我們的報告僅向全體股東作出，不可用                                   auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely
      作 其 他 用 途。吾 等 概 不 就 本 報 告 的 內 容 向 任                    to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume
      何其他人士負上或承擔任何責任。                                        responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the
                                                             contents of this report.


      合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按香港                                   Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a
      審計準則進行的審計在某一重大錯誤陳述存                                    guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will
      在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳 述 可 以 由 欺 詐 或 錯 誤                    always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements
      引 起，倘 合 理 預 期 其 個 別 或 匯 總 起 來 可 能 影                    can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,
      響使用者根據該等綜合財務報表所作出的經                                    individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected
      濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                                    to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of
                                                             these consolidated financial statements.


      在 根 據 香 港 審 計 準 則 進 行 審 計 的 過 程 中，吾                    As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
      等運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。吾                                   professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism
      等亦：                                                    throughout the audit. We also:


      •     識別及評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                                 •     Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
            合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，                                      consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or
            設計及執行審計程序以應對該等風險，                                      error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to
            以及取得充足且適當的審計憑證，作為                                      those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and
            吾等意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串                                      appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not
            謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕                                     detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher
            於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐                                      than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion,
            而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險比因錯誤                                      forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the
            而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險更高。                                       override of internal control.

         該等財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會                                       These financial statements have been prepared in
        （「香 港 會 計 師 公 會」）頒 佈 之 香 港 財 務                          accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards
         報告準則（「香港財務報告準則」，其包                                     (“HKFRSs”) (which include all Hong Kong Financial Reporting
         括所有香港財務報告準則、香港會計準                                      Standards, Hong Kong Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and
         則（「香港會計準則」）及詮釋）、香港公                                    Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified
         認會計原則及香港公司條例的披露規定                                      Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting principles generally
         編製。財務報表乃按歷史成本慣例法編                                      accepted in Hong Kong and the disclosure requirements of the
         製，惟按公允值計量且變動計入損益之                                      Hong Kong Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared
         金融資產按公允值計量除外。除非另有                                      under the historical cost convention except for financial assets
         註明，否則此等財務報表以人民幣（「人                                     at fair value through profit or loss which have been measured at
         民幣」）呈列，所有數值均按四捨五入法                                     fair value. These financial statements are presented in Renminbi
         調整至最接近千位。                                              (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the nearest thousand
                                                                except when otherwise indicated.


         綜合基準                                                   Basis of consolidation
         綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司                                      The consolidated financial statements include the financial
        （ 統 稱「本 集 團」）截 至 二 零 一 八 年 十 二                          statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively
         月三十一日止年度之財務報表。附屬公                                      referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December
         司乃由本公司直接或間接控制之實體                                       2018. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),
        （ 包括結構性實體）。當本集團就參與投                                     directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control
         資對象營運所得之可變回報承受風險或                                      is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to
         享有權利，且擁有運用其對投資對象之                                      variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has
         權力影響該等回報的能力（ 即賦予本集                                     the ability to affect those returns through its power over the
         團指導投資對象相關業務現時能力之現                                      investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current
         有權利）時，即表明擁有控制權。                                        ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).


         倘本公司直接或間接擁有少於投資對象                                      When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less than a
         大多數投票或類似權利的權利，則本集                                      majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group
         團於評估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力                                      considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
         時會考慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：                                      whether it has power over an investee, including:

   附屬公司的財務報表乃使用一致的會計                    The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared
   政策，並按與本公司相同的報告期間編                    for the same reporting period as the Company, using
   製。附屬公司的業績已由本集團取得控                    consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are
   制權當日綜合入賬，並將繼續合併直至                    consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains control,
   該等控制權終止當日為止。                         and continue to be consolidated until the date that such control
                                        ceases.


   損益及其他全面收益各個組成部分仍歸                    Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive
   屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非控制性                     income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group
   權益，即使此舉導致非控制性權益為負                    and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the
   數。因本集團成員公司間交易而產生的                    non-controlling interests having a deficit balance. All intra-
   所有集團內公司間資產及負債、權益、                    group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash
   收入、開支及現金流量均於合併時悉數                    flows relating to transactions between members of the Group
   對銷。                                  are eliminated in full on consolidation.


   倘有事實及情況顯示上文附屬公司所述                    The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
   的三個控制權元素中，有一個或以上出                    facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
   現變動，則本集團會重新評估是否控制                    or more of the three elements of control described above. A
   投資實體。一家附屬公司之所有權權益                    change in the ownership interest of a subsidiary, without a loss
   發 生 變 動（ 並 無 失 去 控 制 權）按 權 益 交       of control, is accounted for as an equity transaction.
   易入賬。


   倘本集團失去對一家附屬公司之控制                     If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it derecognises (i)
   權，則 其 撤 銷 確 認(i)該 附 屬 公 司 之 資 產      the assets (including goodwill) and liabilities of the subsidiary,
  （ 包括商譽）及負債、(ii)任何非控制性權                (ii) the carrying amount of any non-controlling interest and (iii)
   益之賬面值及(iii)於權益內記錄之累計換                the cumulative translation differences recorded in equity; and
   算 差 額；及 確 認(i)所 收 代 價 之 公 允 值、       recognises (i) the fair value of the consideration received, (ii)
   (ii)所保留任何投資之公允值及(iii)損益賬             the fair value of any investment retained and (iii) any resulting
   中任何因此產生之盈餘或虧損。先前於                    surplus or deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share of
   其他全面收益表內確認之本集團應佔部                    components previously recognised in other comprehensive
   份，會按就本集團直接出售相關資產或                    income is reclassified to profit or loss or retained profits, as
   負債規定的相同基準重新分類為損益或                    appropriate, on the same basis as would be required if the

      2.2 會計政策變動及披露                                       2.2 CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND
                                                              DISCLOSURES
         本集團於本年度之財務報表首次採納以                                      The Group has adopted the following new and revised HKFRSs

                香港財務報告準則第9號將香港會                                       HKFRS 9 replaces the “incurred loss” model in HKAS 39
                計準則第39號的「已產生虧損」模                                      with the “expected credit loss” (“ECL”) model. The ECL
                式替換為「預期信貸虧損」 （「預                                      model requires an ongoing measurement of credit risk
                期信貸虧損」）模式。預期信貸虧損                                      associated with a financial asset and therefore recognises
                模式要求持續計量與金融資產相                                        ECLs earlier than under the “incurred loss” accounting
                關的信貸風險，因此在此模式下確                                       model in HKAS 39. The Group applies the new ECL
                認預期信貸虧損會較香港會計準                                        model to all financial assets measured at amortised cost.
                則 第39號 的 已 產 生 虧 損 會 計 模 式
                為早。本集團將新的預期信貸虧損
                模式應用於以攤銷成本計算的金
                融資產。


                本 集 團 已 進 行 詳 細 分 析，考 慮 所                             The Group performed a detailed analysis which considers
                有 合 理 及 輔 助 資 料（ 包 括 前 瞻 因                            all reasonable and supportable information, including
                素），以根據香港財務報告準則第                                       forward-looking elements, for estimation of ECL on its
                9號的規定估計其金融資產的預期                                       financial assets in accordance with the requirements
                信貸虧損。採納香港財務報告準則                                       of HKFRS 9. The adoption of HKFRS 9 has had no
                第9號並無對本集團的金融資產減                                       significant impact on the impairment of the financial
                值構成重大影響。概無其他信貸虧                                       assets of the Group. No additional credit loss allowance
                損撥備已就累計溢利作出確認，因                                       has been recognised against accumulated profits as the

      2.3 已頒佈但尚未生效的香港財務                                   2.3 ISSUED BUT NOT YET EFFECTIVE
          報告準則                                                HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING

         本集團並無於此等財務報表中應用下列                                      The Group has not applied the following new and revised

2.4 主要會計政策概要                         2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
                                         POLICIES
   於聯營公司之投資                            Investment in an associate
   聯營公司為本集團於其中擁有長期權益                   An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term
  （ 一般不少於20%股本投票權），並可藉                 interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting
   此對其行使重大影響力之實體。重大影                   rights and over which it is in a position to exercise significant
   響力為參與被投資公司財務及經營政策                   influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
   決定之權力，惟對該等政策並無控制或                   financial and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is
   共同控制權。                              not control or joint control over those policies.


   本集團於聯營公司之投資根據權益會計                   The Group’s investment in an associate is stated in the
   法，按本集團應佔資產淨值減任何減值                   consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s
   虧損於綜合財務狀況表列賬。任何可能                   share of net assets under the equity method of accounting,
   存在之相異會計政策已相應作出調整以                   less any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into
   使之貫徹一致。                             line any dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.


   本集團應佔聯營公司收購後業績及其他                   The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other
   全面收益分別計入綜合損益表及綜合全                   comprehensive income of the associate is included in the
   面 收 益 表 內。此 外，當 變 動 直 接 於 聯 營       consolidated statement of profit or loss and consolidated
   公司權益中確認時，本集團會於綜合權                   other comprehensive income, respectively. In addition, when
   益變動表中確認其應佔的任何變動（ 如                  there has been a change recognised directly in the equity of
   適 用）。本 集 團 與 其 聯 營 公 司 間 之 交 易      the associate, the Group recognises its share of any changes,
   所產生之未變現損益乃按本集團於聯營                   when applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes in
   公司之投資對銷，惟有證據證明所轉讓                   equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions
   資產出現減值之未變現虧損則作別論。                   between the Group and its associate are eliminated to the
   收購聯營公司產生之商譽計入本集團於                   extent of the Group’s investments in the associate, except
   聯營公司之投資。                            where unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of
                                       the assets transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of
                                       an associate is included as part of the Group’s investment in
                                       an associate.

   而言，本集團選擇按公允值或所佔被收                control of the acquiree. For each business combination, the

   人權利於清盤時按比例分佔淨資產的非                their holders to a proportionate share of net assets in the event

   當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約                When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial

   公允值計量                               Fair value measurement
   本集團於各報告期末計量其投資於金融                   The Group measures its investments in financial products at
   產品的公允值。公允值為市場參與者間                   fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the
   於計量日期進行的有序交易中，出售資                   price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer
   產所收取或轉讓負債所支付之價格。公                   a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants
   允值計量假設出售資產或轉讓負債的交                   at the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based
   易於該資產或負債的主要市場，或在無                   on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or
   主要市場的情況下，則於對該資產或負                   transfer the liability takes place either in the principal market
   債最有利的市場進行。本集團必須可於                   for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal market,
   該主要市場或最有利市場進行交易。資                   in the most advantageous market for the asset or liability.
   產或負債的公允值乃採用市場參與者為                   The principal or the most advantageous market must be
   資產或負債定價時所用的假設計量，即                   accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability
   假設市場參與者按其最佳經濟利益行                    is measured using the assumptions that market participants
   事。                                  would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that
                                       market participants act in their economic best interest.

   預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就初步                  ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for

   敞口而言，會為未來12個月可能發生違                 since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses

   貸虧損撥備（12個月預期信貸虧損）。就                12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for
   初步確認以來信貸風險大幅增加的信貸                  which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since
   敞口而言，須就預期於敝口的餘下年期                  initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
   產生的信貸虧損計提減值撥備，不論違                  expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of
   約的時間（ 整個可使用年期預期信貸虧                 the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
   損）。


   於報告日期，本集團評估一項金融工具                  At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the
   的信貸風險自初步確認以來是否大幅增                  credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly
   加。於 作 出 評 估 時，本 集 團 將 金 融 工 具      since initial recognition. When making the assessment,
   於報告日期發生違約的風險與金融工具                  the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the

   前瞻性資料）。                            information that is available without undue cost or effort,

   金融資產減值（ 二零一八年一月一日前                  Impairment of financial assets (policies under HKAS 39
   適用之香港會計準則第39號下之政策）                  applicable before 1 January 2018)
   本集團於每個報告期末作出評核有否客                   The Group assesses at the end of each reporting period
   觀證據，證明某項金融資產或金融資產                   whether there is objective evidence that a financial asset or a
   組別已減值。倘於初始確認資產後發生                   group of financial assets is impaired. An impairment exists if
   之一項或多項事件對可作可靠估量之某                   one or more events that occurred after the initial recognition of
   項金融資產或金融資產組別之估計未來                   the asset have an impact on the estimated future cash flows
   現 金 流 量 產 生 影 響，則 存 在 減 值。減 值       of the financial asset or the group of financial assets that can
   證據可包括一個或多個債務人面臨重大                   be reliably estimated. Evidence of impairment may include
   財政困難、拖欠或未能償還利息或本金                   indications that a debtor or a group of debtors is experiencing
   款額、彼等可能破產或進行其他財務重                   significant financial difficulty, default or delinquency in interest
   組及可觀察數據顯示預計未來現金流量                   or principal payments, the probability that they will enter
   將嚴重減少，例如欠款數目變動或出現                   bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation and observable
   與違約相關的經濟狀況。                         data indicating that there is a measurable decrease in the
                                       estimated future cash flows, such as changes in arrears or
                                       economic conditions that correlate with defaults.


  以攤銷成本列賬之金融資產                         Financial assets carried at amortised cost

             之賬面值超過其可收回金額（ 即其公允                                     be recoverable. An impairment exists when the carrying value
             值減出售成本與其使用價值兩者之較高                                      of an asset or a cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable
             者）時，即為出現減值。公允值減出售成                                     amount, which is the higher of its fair value less costs of
             本乃根據來自類似資產之公平交易中具                                      disposal and its value in use. The calculation of the fair value

             來 現 金 流 量，並 選 擇 合 適 之 貼 現 率，以                          undertaken, management must estimate the expected future

         被視為悉數行使或轉換為普通股而按零                                      the weighted average number of ordinary shares assumed to

         本公司董事認為，由於信貸質素並無重                                      looking information, the directors of the Company were of

           根據中國公司法及中國大陸附屬                         In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC and the
           公司之組織章程細則，各中國大陸                        articles of association of the Mainland China subsidiaries,
           附屬公司須將按照中國公認會計                         each of the Mainland China subsidiaries is required to
           原則釐定之除稅後利潤10%撥入至                       allocate 10% of their profit after tax, as determined in
           法定盈餘公積金，直至有關公積金                        accordance with the PRC generally accepted accounting
           達到註冊股本之50%為止。部分法                       principles, to the SSR until the reserve reaches 50% of
           定盈餘公積金可用於增加繳足股                         its registered capital. Part of the SSR may be converted
           本，惟資本化後之餘額不得少於註                        to increase paid-up capital, provided that the remaining
           冊股本之25%。                               balance after the capitalisation is not less than 25% of the
                                                  registered capital.

   應付賬款。                                payables, which arise directly from its operations.

   東發還資本或發行新股。截至二零一八                    adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital
   年十二月三十一日及二零一七年十二                     to shareholders or issue new shares. No changes were made
   月三十一日止年度，有關管理資本的目                    in the objectives, policies or processes for managing capital
   標、政策或程序並無變動。                         during the years ended 31 December 2018 and 31 December
                                        2017.

   以及其他應付款項及應計款項，減現金                    other payables and accruals, less cash and cash equivalents.
   及現金等價物。資本包括可換股債券負                    Capital includes the liability component of convertible bonds
